- [ ] **Step 8: Write the finish report**

The contract asks that rulings and friction be recorded. This lane's are:

0. **The ledger's rulings, and where each landed.** The race gap (ruling 1) is Task 12; the talent converter (ruling 2) is Task 16, consumed by Task 12's `CombatantBuilder`; the buffs mapping (the web refresh's ruling) is Task 7; the fight-close scope stays the contract's (a signed-in member). Say which of them the lane actually exercised end to end, since two are blocked on data that does not exist yet.
1. **`api/internal/ids` does not exist.** `sim_id` uses `auth.Base32ID(auth.ReportIDChars)` from `api/internal/auth/ids.go`, the same generator, alphabet and length `report_id` uses. Ruled, not changed.
2. **The migration is 0013, not 0006.** The contract's Postgres section was written when 0005 was the latest; `main` is at 0012. Same statements, different number.
3. **`GET /v1/characters/{character_key}/sim-input` is mounted as three path segments**, `{region}/{ruleset}/{name}`, because that is how `rankings` already mounts the character route.
4. **Armory is not yet one of `sim-input`'s sources**: nothing in this repository stores an Armory refresh. The addon export and the character's newest ranked fight are. The response shape does not change when Armory joins.
5. **The run endpoint and the R2 layout follow the contract as amended on `main` `fa2cac2`:** a plain JSON `SimRequest`, no `Raw`, no `request.bin`; the queued row holds the request and only `sims/<sim_id>/result.json` crosses through R2. Nothing further is asked of the contract here; say so, so the next reader knows the amendment landed.
6. **The execution score cannot be computed yet, and the reason is data, not code.** A fight's `summary.CombatantRow` has gear, talent ids, consumables and raid buffs; `simapi.CharacterSpec` also needs a race (COMBATANT_INFO records only a faction number, and nothing else here stores one) and the engine's positional talent string. `sims.NoBuilder` is the seam, `execution_score` stays null, and the open question goes to the controller.
7. **`worst_actions` rows carry a `spell_id` the contract's `{name, sim_casts, actual_casts}` does not name.** It is the summary's row identity, and without it the sim's rows (`"spell:23881"`) and a log's (`"Bloodthirst"`) cannot be joined at all. Additive; the web reads the name as before.
8. **`sim-validate` no longer degrades on a missing `data/curated/specs.json`.** The contract says it should measure nothing and say so; the list is compiled in through `sim/specs` now, so it cannot be missing. The behaviour that clause protected — never crash for want of a spec list — still holds.
9. **`sim-input`'s `buffs` are engine ids mapped from recorded spell ids, and an unmapped id is dropped with a logged reason.** The contract names the field but not its vocabulary; `IDS.md` is the vocabulary, and the drop is visible in the logs rather than silent in the number.
10. **Whether `forever-sim` was in the image** when the lane finished (Task 15), and therefore whether any sim in this lane ran the real engine or the fixture.
11. **Whether the Docker image build (Step 4) and the whole-module `sim` test run (Step 1) were run**, and if not, why.

```bash
cd /Users/jh/code/forever/.worktrees/sim-api
git add api/internal/sims api/internal/reports api/internal/rankings sim/runner sim/talents
git commit -m "test(api): cover the simulator's error paths to the 80% floor" \
  -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```

Skip this step rather than making an empty commit if the review changed nothing.

**Gaps found while reviewing, and closed inline:**

- The contract's API table said `POST /v1/sims/run` takes a `SimRequest` "with `Raw` set". The contract was amended on `main` `fa2cac2` and now says a plain JSON `SimRequest` with no `request.bin`, which is what Tasks 3, 5 and 11 write.
- Nothing said how the `api` module reaches the engine. Probed twice: the engine-free packages of `sim/` import into another module with no engine in the graph and no `go.sum` entry, and they build, vet and test with `GOWORK=off` even when `sim/go.mod`'s engine `replace` points at a path that does not exist. Written into Global Constraints, used by the CI step in Task 8, checked in Task 14 Step 3.
- Nothing put `forever-sim` in the image, so `simEngine()` could never return `Native`. Task 15 now builds it there from the pin, and the deploy fails rather than shipping an image without it.
- The design wants a card per spec on the support page; an earlier draft returned only measured rows, so the page was empty until the first nightly run. Task 6 overlays measurements on `sim/specs`.
- Adding `execution` to `Metrics` would have widened the percentile gate too, turning a 400 into a silent "no percentile". Task 9 splits the list.
- `sim-input` named `buffs` and never filled it, and left `talents` empty on the addon branch. Task 7 fills both from the fight the character last parsed, and states — with a test — that an export-only character has gear and no talents, because the export is opaque to this repository.
- An earlier draft widened fight-close scoring to every DPS parse on a ranked report. Withdrawn: the contract's "signed-in member" stands, and Task 12 implements it with one query per fight.
- The role rule was written twice, once in each package. Now once, in `sims`: the ingest hands over the row's role and `Score` drops what the simulator does not model.
- `sims` must not import `reports` and `reports` must not import `sims`, or the graph cycles. `reports.Scorer`, `reports.Members`, `sims.FightAt` and the `scorerShim` in `main.go` keep them apart, with a struct conversion the compiler checks.
- The scorer was placed "beside the sampler", where `deps.Ingest` is still nil. Task 12 pins it after the assignment and adds a wiring test, because a nil interface field compiles perfectly well and silently scores nothing.

### 2. Placeholder scan

Searched the plan for every pattern in the skill's list: `TBD`, `TODO`, `implement later`, `fill in details`, `add appropriate error handling`, `add validation`, `handle edge cases`, `write tests for the above`, `similar to Task N`. None present. Every code step carries real, compiling code and every test step carries a real test.

The four places that could have been placeholders are not:

- `runner.Fixture` is a complete, tested implementation whose summary is built from `logs/engine/summary`'s own types, so the compiler checks it.
- `sims.NoBuilder` and `sims.CombatantBuilder` are both complete: the second assembles gear, level, talents, buffs and consumables and then names the one field nothing records, with `file:line` evidence for each claim about what a log does and does not carry.
- `sim/talents` is a whole package with a fixture, five tests and a sixth that runs against whatever real build the checkout has, without naming one.
- Task 3's temporary `Mount` and Task 4's `pending.go` are named as scaffolds, are deleted by the exact later task that replaces each one, and each says which task that is.

Two prose instructions are deliberately not literal code, and both name the file to look in: Task 9's "reuse the package's existing `newHarness`/`seedReport`/`seedFight`/`get`", and Task 12's "check `(*Store).Get`'s name in this package before using it". Duplicating those harnesses would be worse than pointing at them. Task 12's ingest tests no longer hedge about a helper that does not exist: they add `postVerifiedFight` over the package's own `makeBundle` idiom.

### 3. Name consistency with the contract and with the `sim` module's real exports

Checked every name that crosses a task or a lane boundary, against the files as of `sim-engine` `4166eb0`, `main` `01744f5`, and the fork at `9326fffd9`.

- From `sim/api`: `SimRequest`, `CharacterSpec`, `GearSlot`, `CharacterSource`, `EncounterSpec`, `SimResult`, `Estimate`, `DefaultEncounter()`, `Validate() error`, `Stale`, `ValidIterations`, `LaneBrowser`, `LaneServer`, `SourceAddon`, `SourceFight`, `SourceManual`, `MaxTargets`, `SimLevel`. Used with those exact spellings in Tasks 1, 3, 4, 5, 10, 11, 12, 13. The names the first draft invented — `WithDefaults`, `IterationsDefault`, `Sources`, `Raw`, a `map[string]string` from `Validate` — appear nowhere.
- From `sim/enginever`: `Version`. Read in `main.go`, in `testEngine`, and by the image's engine stage; nothing else spells a sha.
- From `sim/specs`: `All`, `Spec{Spec, ClassSlug, SpecSlug, Name, Role, TreeIndex}`. Used by `DPSSpecs` (Task 1) and nowhere else.
- From `sim/request`: nothing is imported — it imports the engine. Its `IDS.md` is read as a **file** by Task 7's vocabulary test, which is the only way the `api` module can check itself against that list.
- From `sim/runner` (this lane's, Task 10): `Runner`, `Progress`, `Native`, `Fixture`, `DefaultBinary`, `ErrBadInput`. Called with three arguments in Tasks 11, 12, 13; `ErrBadInput` is compared with `errors.Is` in Task 11's test and is the one failure the job never retries.
- From `sim/talents` (this lane's, Task 16): `Layouts`, `Load`, `String`, `Classes`, `ErrUnknownClass`, `ErrUnknownTalent`, `ErrTooManyRanks`. Consumed by Task 12's `CombatantBuilder` and loaded once in `main.go`.
- Import alias: `simapi` for `github.com/jhunthrop/foreversixty/sim/api` in every `api`-module file; plain `api` inside the `sim` module. Consistent in Tasks 1, 3, 4, 5, 10, 11, 12, 13.
- `Store.Save(ctx, id string, userID *int64, title string, res simapi.SimResult) error` — declared Task 3; called Task 4 as `Save(r.Context(), id, owner, trimTitle(in.Title), in.SimResult)` where `owner` is `*int64`. Matches.
- `Store.Queue(ctx, id string, userID int64, req simapi.SimRequest) error` — declared Task 3; called Task 5 as `Queue(r.Context(), id, actor.UserID, req)`. Non-pointer because a server run always has an owner.
- `Store.Get`, `Mine`, `Progress`, `Advance`, `Finish`, `Fail`, `Specs`, `PutSpec`, `Validated`, `SimInput` — declared in Tasks 3, 6 and 7, each listed in the Produces of the task that writes it, and called in Tasks 4, 5, 7, 11, 12, 13 with matching arities.
- `Keys{SimID: …}.Result()` and `ResultPut` — declared Task 3; used in Tasks 7 (the test's summary write) and 11. There is no `Keys.Request()`: no request object exists.
- `Getter`, `fightSummary`, `combatantNamed`, `MaxSummaryBytes` — declared once, in Task 7's `summaries.go`, and consumed by Task 13's `ParseReader`. The earlier draft declared the reader twice, in two files.
- `Premiumer` — declared once, in `run.go`: as the bare interface in Task 4's scaffold and in full in Task 5's replacement of that same file. No duplicate declaration, because Task 5 replaces rather than appends. `run.go` is named in Task 4's Files list.
- `SimRunJobCommand` — declared once in `handler.go` (Task 4); used in `run.go` (Task 5), the run test (Task 5) and `main.go` (Task 11). `ValidateJobCommand` — declared once in `validate.go` (Task 13); used in `main.go` (Task 13).
- `Service` fields `Store`, `Accounts`, `Jobs`, `Summaries`, `EngineVersion`, `Log` — declared in Tasks 4 and 7, set by the harness in Tasks 5 and 7 and by `main.go` in Task 8. There is no `Objects` field: the bucket write belongs to the job's `JobDeps`, because no HTTP handler writes an object.
- `Builder.FightCharacter(spec, class string, c summary.CombatantRow) (simapi.CharacterSpec, error)` — declared Task 12; implemented by `NoBuilder`, `CombatantBuilder` and the test's `alwaysBuilds`; called in Tasks 12 and 13 with the same argument order. It returns the envelope's character, not protobuf bytes.
- `Scores.SetExecutionScore(ctx, reportID string, fightIndex int, playerKey string, score float64) error` — declared Task 12; satisfied by `rankings.Store.SetExecutionScore` written in Task 9 with exactly that signature; called in Tasks 12 and 13.
- `Members.MemberKeys(ctx, keys []string) (map[string]bool, error)` — declared in `reports` (Task 12); satisfied by `auth.Store.MemberKeys`, written in the same task; the test's `fakeMembers` has the same shape.
- The two fight-close structs have identical fields in identical order, which is what makes the conversion compile: `reports.ScoredFight` → `sims.FightAt` → `sims.ScoreTask`. Field by field: `ReportID string`, `FightIndex int`, `PlayerKey string`, `Spec string`, `Class string`, `Role string`, `ActualDPS float64`, `DurationSec int`, `Combatant summary.CombatantRow`. The compiler is the drift check.
- `StateFor(medianGap float64, parses int) string` — declared Task 6; called Task 13 as `StateFor(gap, f.Parses)`.
- `worstOf(casts map[int64]*WorstAction, parses int) []WorstAction` and `foldCasts(map[int64]*WorstAction, summary.Summary, Parse)` — declared Task 13; its own tests call both with those signatures. `Parse.ActualCasts` is `map[int64]CastCount`, and `castsBy` returns exactly that.
- `WorstAction{SpellID int64, Name string, SimCasts, ActualCasts float64}` — declared Task 6, written by Task 13, documented in Task 8's OpenAPI. Both sides of the comparison key on `SpellID`, never on the name.
- `SpecFidelity.MedianGap` is `*float64` everywhere, so "unmeasured" is null and never a zero gap; `ExecutionScore` is `*float64` on all three row types (Task 9), so "no score" is null everywhere it appears.
- `DPSSpecs` is exported from Task 1, where it is written, because `cmd/api/main.go` calls it in Task 13; no task renames it mid-flight. `roleDPS` is declared once, in Task 1, and used by `Score` and by `parses.go`'s query.
- `ErrNotFound` is `sims.ErrNotFound` (Task 3), distinct from `reports.ErrNotFound`; every handler compares with `errors.Is` against its own package's.
- `errAnyway` and `testEngine` are declared once each, in `harness_test.go` (Task 3), and reused by Tasks 5, 7, 11, 12, 13's tests. `fakeScores`, `alwaysBuilds`, `aTask` and `h.validate` are declared once each in `score_test.go` (Task 12) and reused by `validate_test.go` (Task 13) — same package, so no duplicate declaration. `dirGetter` is declared once, in Task 7's `input_test.go`.
